StatiCrypt Password protect a static HTML page StatiCrypt uses AES-256 with WebCrypto to encrypt your html string with your long password, in your browser (client side). Download your encrypted string in a HTML page with a password prompt you can upload anywhere (see example). The tool is also available as a CLI on NPM and is open source on GitHub. ► HOW IT WORKS Password •••••••••• HTML/string to encrypt Personal Dashboard
